# The Predictive Universe: Structural Walkthrough

## Executive Summary

The Predictive Universe (PU) framework begins from the Cogito-certified occurrence of awareness and represents knowledge-bearing activity by a predictive operational normal form on the finite self-verifying class stated in Appendix P. Matter, MPUs, quantum response, spacetime, gravity, Standard-Model structure, parameter candidates, and spectral calibration follow only on their named physicalization, carrier, reconstruction, realization, continuum, calibration, and empirical certificate intersections. The three operational axioms and PPI/PCE comparison rules organize those branches but do not by themselves derive the downstream physical sectors. Appendix A.6 and Appendix P.16c supply the conservative generative-non-closure reading: a trace-certified predictor has no total standard-sound same-cycle Boolean closure on a domain containing its targeted live diagonal sentence. A stable finite partial-closure boundary follows only for prediction-update cycles satisfying every hypothesis of Theorem A.6.4, including finite-budget retention, binary verification, verification-preserving composition, logical memory, uniform specification, and the live-diagonal guard; external or historical processing of the target sentence remains possible. PPI is used here as an operational instantiation rule: finite protocol-response presheaves define physical content, response-null labels are quotiented, and PCE selects the least-cost representative in the admissible class. Rows depending on validation targets, bridge laws, reference prefactors, model kernels, or uncomputed spectral data are carried with their status labels and are not counted as theorem-level predictions until the corresponding strict certificate gates are closed. Rows carrying an accepted finite spectral calibration datum $\mathfrak S_*$ and the corresponding spectral-calibration registry record $\zeta$ are final-calibrated: their continuous coefficients are determined as branch-scaled spectral moments of the unique $\omega_*$ of Principle V.3.11b.

**Map-entry convention.** Every bold heading in this file that reproduces a typed manuscript label (Axiom, Definition, Lemma, Proposition, Theorem, Corollary, Hypothesis, Postulate, Remark, Example, or Convention) is a non-authoritative summary and cross-reference. The unique formal declaration is the item in the cited manuscript source; this map declares no formal object and assigns no label or number.

**Joint conditional discrete backbone.** On the conjunction of the registered binary-quotient branch, Theorem 15's (O1)–(O3), (FC) branch, the Hilbert-distinguishability and Theorem-Z.2 minimality branch, and every separately cited downstream active-kernel, code, and mode-channel branch, the following tuple
$$
\varepsilon_0=\ln2,\qquad K_0=3,\qquad N_{\mathrm{vis}}^{\min}=8,\qquad d_0=8,\qquad a=2,\qquad b=6,\qquad M=24,\qquad k=12,\qquad D=4
$$
is available as a conjunction of branch conclusions, not as an implication chain from SPAP:
$$
\begin{gathered}
\text{registered binary quotient}\Rightarrow\varepsilon_0=\ln2,\\
(\mathrm{O1})\text{--}(\mathrm{O3})+(\mathrm{FC})
\Rightarrow (N_{\mathrm{vis}}^{\min},K_0)=(8,3),\\
\text{Hilbert distinguishability + Theorem-Z.2 minimality hypotheses}
\Rightarrow d_0=8,\\
\text{separately registered downstream branches}
\Rightarrow (a,b,M,k,D)=(2,6,24,12,4).
\end{gathered}
$$
Here $N_{\mathrm{vis}}^{\min}=8$ is the least visited-context count only on Theorem 15's (O1)–(O3), (FC) class. Perfect Hilbert distinguishability gives only $d_0\ge8$; equality additionally requires Theorem Z.2's admissible eight-state comparator, response-preserving quotientability, strict support-cost monotonicity, and minimal-branch hypotheses. On the additional finite-response positive-marginal full-block-frame, faithfulness, determinant/center-response, and tensor-category reconstruction branch, the inactive-sector partition $6=3+2+1$ gives the conditional Lie algebra
$$
\mathfrak g_{\mathrm{SM}}=\mathfrak{su}(3)\oplus\mathfrak{su}(2)\oplus\mathfrak u(1)
$$
The Global Strict-Certificate Ledger of Convention P.14.1k records the closed backbone alongside the diagnostic-only alpha branch $\mathfrak C_{\alpha,YR\perp}$ and the remaining certificate-pending, branch-discharge, current-source negative, or model-layer sectors $\mathfrak Z_{\mathrm{cont}}$, $\mathfrak P_{\mathrm{AQFT}}$, $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{EH}}$, $\mathfrak F_U^{(4)}$, $\mathfrak D_Q$, $\mathfrak R_{\mathrm{RHG}}$, $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{fl}}$, $\mathfrak J_{\mathrm{RHG-fl}}$, $\mathfrak C_B$, $\mathfrak C_B^{\mathrm{tr}}$, $\mathfrak C_B^{\mathrm{APSK}}$, $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{gen}}$, $\mathfrak S_{\mathrm{hor},n}$, $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{Hdesign}}$, $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{PageTV}}$, $\mathfrak T_{\mathrm{hor}}$, the finite nuclear Hamiltonian, Environmental SPAP hard-core Gibbs data, the dark-response certificate $\mathfrak X_{\mathrm{DS}}$, and the cross-sector numerical record $\mathfrak N_{\mathrm{PU}}$; Theorem P.14.1k.1 fixes a compatible selected sector tuple only under acceptance and overlap-map commutativity; one ambient global realizer requires the separate existence and uniqueness conditions stated in the scope paragraph following that theorem.

### Framework Status Ledger

The framework begins with the Cogito as an epistemic starting point, and formalizes prediction via the PU axioms to derive constraints on physical structure. The table below shows both the philosophical foundation and the derived operational mechanisms.

## Quick-Start Guide: The Essential Derivation in 7 Steps

For newcomers, the argument begins from awareness and models finite-resource knowing as prediction, checking, and revision. An MPU is an attained least-cost response-equivalence class only for a registered task whose comparison and attainment gates close. Separately, the minimal rank-two $d_0=8$ carrier has $M=24$ QFI-active tangent generators; on Definition Z.9a's faithful tangent-shell branch, Theorems Z.10--Z.11 select $D_{\rm car}=4$ as the least feasible Euclidean response-carrier dimension. Neither statement identifies twenty-four boundary responses of a universal simplest predictor, and promotion to physical spacetime retains its continuum and Lorentzian certificates.

### Technical seven-step ledger

| Step | What | Why | Result |
|:----:|:-----|:----|:-------|
| **1** | **Cogito** — Conscious awareness exists | Self-evident; cannot be coherently denied | Foundation established |
| **2** | **Predictive operation** — finite-resource knowing is operationally modeled by predictive update | The Cogito certifies awareness; Definition P.3.1 supplies the predictive-operation structure when awareness performs state updates | Prediction as operational primitive |
| **3** | **SPAP** — No universally exact predictor exists on the stated diagonal class | Two independent routes: the diagonal obstruction of Theorem 10 under its finite-program representation, closure, and diagonal-use hypotheses, and the unfolded-representation regress of Theorem 4.2.6c under well-foundedness | No universal scalar $\alpha_{\mathrm{SPAP}}$ follows |
| **4** | **Structural binary value and registered-reset bound** | Definition 28 and Definition J.1 register the binary quotient, and Theorem J.1 gives $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$; a physical reset satisfying the conditional Landauer hypotheses separately gives $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{reset}}\ge H_q(P\mid R)$ | Structural and heat ledgers remain distinct; SPAP alone supplies neither reset premise |
| **5** | **$a = 2$** — Physical instantiation | PPI + PCE: sharp match/mismatch active record plus minimal integer $a$ satisfying $\ln a \ge \varepsilon$ | Active kernel dimension on the attractor branch (Theorem Z.1) |
| **6** | **$M = 24$** — Interface mode count | QFI structure: $M = 2ab = 2 \times 2 \times 6 = 24$ on the minimal $d_0 = 8$ branch | Determines the Appendix Z backbone (Theorem Z.5) |
| **7** | **$D=4$** — Euclidean response-carrier dimension | The faithful shell gives $24\le K(D)$; $K(3)=12$ excludes lower dimensions; the regular $24$-cell and strict surplus-dimension cost select the least feasible four | Exact on Definition Z.9a's tangent-shell branch; the $3+1$ spacetime interpretation separately requires continuum, principal-symbol, time-orientation, and metric-reconstruction certificates (Theorems Z.10-Z.11; Corollary P.8.3) |

**The Branch Ledger:**
$$

\boxed{
\begin{gathered}
(\mathrm{O1})\text{--}(\mathrm{O3})+(\mathrm{FC})
\Rightarrow K_0=3,\quad N_{\mathrm{vis}}^{\min}=8,\\
\text{perfect Hilbert distinguishability}\Rightarrow d_0\ge8,\qquad
\text{Theorem-Z.2 minimal branch}\Rightarrow d_0=8,\\
\text{binary fixed-ready reset}\Rightarrow\varepsilon_0=\ln2,\qquad
\text{Theorem-Z.1 branch}\Rightarrow a=2,\\
\text{Theorem-Z.5 branch}\Rightarrow M=24,\qquad
\text{faithful-shell least-feasible branch}\Rightarrow D=4.
\end{gathered}
}
$$

**Key Bridge Principle:**

> **Summary of Definition P.6.2 (Principle of Physical Instantiation — PPI):** Any derivable, self-consistent logical or mathematical structure, when physically instantiated by a system composed of finite resources and operating in finite time, will manifest in the physical world with properties and dynamics shaped by the irreducible thermodynamic costs inherent in its implementation.

> **Note (PPI, PCE, and attractor gates):** PPI registers finite physical representatives and their response-active costs; PCE declares the objective on a specified admissible comparison class. Their conjunction alone proves neither existence nor dynamical attainment. A PCE-attractor requires Definition 15a's attained infimum, nonempty global-minimizer set, singleton quotient orbit, flat U(1)-sector SLD-QFI, and cap-active constrained minimum, together with the stability or convergence certificate for the declared adaptation law.

*PPI requires response-active distinctions to have physical representatives; it does not convert the conditional reset bound into an abstract logical necessity. On Theorem Z.1's sharp-record, entropy-capacity, and no-surplus branch, the structural value $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$ participates in selecting $a=2$. Equality of physical reset heat with $\ln2$ is a separate conditionally uniform, zero-overhead statement and does not fix $a$.*

## Part I: Axiomatic Foundation

### The Three Axioms

**Summary of Axiom 1 (Ax 1): The Prediction Optimization Problem (POP)** [§2.1]

Adaptive systems operating within uncertain environments face the fundamental challenge of optimizing the predictive quality (Q) of their internal state or model concerning aspects of their internal and external environment relevant to their continued functioning and goals. This optimization aims to maximize the expected improvement in predictive quality (ΔQ), quantified by metrics reflecting reduced uncertainty, enhanced accuracy, or reduced Bayes risk, subject to limited physical and computational resources including available energy, processing time, and achievable system complexity (C_P).

**Summary of Axiom 2 (Ax 2): Predictive Capacity Foundation** [§2.2]

To generate predictions demonstrably better than chance (ΔQ > 0), a system must possess an internal model (M_t) capable of capturing and exploiting discoverable regularities within the relevant data streams. The capacity for effective prediction relies fundamentally on the system's ability to leverage Information (Definition 1) via its internal modeling processes to achieve a positive expected improvement in predictive quality with respect to future states pertinent to its operational goals.

**Summary of Axiom 3 (Ax 3): Operational Viability** [§3.3.6]

The process modeled by the adaptive Fundamental Predictive Loop (Definition 4), subject to POP (Axiom 1) and RID (Definition 6) constraints, can only be sustained if its Predictive Performance PP(t) is dynamically maintained within the Space of Becoming, i.e., α < PP(t) < β.

---

### The Compressed Core Dependency Ledger

The framework uses a conjunction of separately registered branches, not one implication chain from SPAP to all downstream physics:

1. **Cogito → predictive operation:** the Cogito certifies awareness as self-verifying epistemic certainty; Definition P.3.1 supplies the finite predictive-operation model.
2. **Predictive operation → SPAP obstruction:** Theorems 10–11 exclude a universally exact predictor on their stated diagonal classes. They do not require a physical merge or reset.
3. **Registered binary fixed-ready reset → structural and heat ledgers:** Definition J.1 and Theorem J.1 give the binary support value $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$ only on the reachable fixed-ready-state reset branch. A physical implementation satisfying the conditional Landauer hypotheses separately obeys $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{reset}}\ge H_q(P\mid R)$; a positive uniform floor and equality with $\ln2$ require their own ensemble and saturation hypotheses.
4. **Full-context register branch:** Theorem 15 gives $K_0=3$ and $N_{\mathrm{vis}}^{\min}=8$ only on the (O1)–(O3), (FC) realization class.
5. **Hilbert and active-kernel branches:** perfect Hilbert distinguishability gives $d_0\ge8$; Theorem Z.2's comparator, quotientability, strict-cost, and minimality hypotheses give $d_0=8$. Theorem Z.1's sharp-record, capacity, and no-surplus hypotheses give $a=2$, hence $b=6$.
6. **Interface and dimension branches:** Theorem Z.5 gives $M=2ab=24$. Definition Z.9a then requires a faithful response-cell injection and strictly penalizes surplus response-null dimensions. Theorem Z.10 gives $24\le K(D)$; $K(3)=12$ excludes lower dimensions; and the regular $24$-cell proves four-dimensional feasibility, so Theorem Z.11 selects the least feasible $D=4$.

Thus the downstream tuple is available on the conjunction of these branches; failure of one bridge does not alter the conclusions of the independent branches.

### Common Misconceptions

| Misconception | Clarification |
|:--------------|:--------------|
| "PU is only an epistemic method, not a consciousness-first ontology" | PU begins with awareness as its point of certainty and develops a consciousness-first account of reality. |
| "$\varepsilon_{\mathrm{reset}}\ge H_q(P\mid R)$ is just Landauer's principle" | The conditional inequality is the registered Landauer reset bound itself. PU separately supplies a reachable binary fixed-ready-state architecture on the Definition-J.1 branch, whose structural log-cardinality is $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$. SPAP does not logically require that architecture, and equality with $k_BT\ln2$ additionally requires a conditionally uniform binary record, no retained copy, and zero excess dissipation. |
| "D = 4 is put in by hand" | The registered $M=24$ modes require a faithful shell; $K(3)=12$ excludes lower dimensions, the regular $24$-cell proves four-dimensional feasibility, and strict surplus-dimension cost selects the least feasible four |
| "The framework is unfalsifiable" | The manuscript contains distinct falsifiers at the theorem, accepted-branch, and open-candidate levels. Examples include the least-feasible $D=4$ tangent-shell theorem, the conditional sinc-core value $\alpha^{-1}_{0}=137.03609205522863\ldots$, and the diagnostic hypercharge-recoil candidate $\alpha^{-1}_{\mathrm{cand}}=137.03599917753023\ldots$. The last row becomes a forward falsifier only after its operator-realization certificate is supplied and frozen before comparison. Other sector targets retain the local hypotheses and status labels stated in their source theorems. |
| "PCE is just entropy maximization" | PCE minimizes *operational cost minus predictive benefit*; it's resource optimization, not thermodynamic equilibration |
| "CC violates quantum mechanics" | CC (< 0.5) modulates ND-RID parameters within allowed bounds; the Born rule structure is preserved |
| "This is just information physics renamed" | PU supplies a typed conditional chain: Theorem 15 fixes $(K_0,N_{\mathrm{vis}}^{\min})=(3,8)$ on its (O1)–(O3), (FC) full-context class; Principle 8.0b and Theorem 8.0d select the faithful complex $d_0=8$ carrier; Theorems Z.1 and Z.5 give $(a,M)=(2,24)$; and the faithful least-feasible shell theorem gives $D=4$. Theorem Z.26 then evaluates a closed-form sinc-core function of those branch data. The $0.678$ ppm comparison is a diagnostic of the core, not proof that the branch assumptions follow from information theory alone. |

### Core Definitions

| Definition | Name | Statement | Reference |
|:-----------|:-----|:----------|:----------|
| **Def 1** | Information | Physically instantiated, substrate-independent pattern with potential to yield positive expected improvement in predictive quality for a task-suitable system and admissible model; no universal $K_0$ floor follows for this general class | §2.3.1 |
| **Def 2** | Prediction-Based Knowledge | Realized capacity for effective prediction yielding ΔQ > 0 | §2.3.2 |
| **Def 3** | Resource Cost Functions | R(C) = physical operational cost; R_I(C) = reflexive-information cost | §2.4.4 |
| **Def 4** | Fundamental Predictive Loop | Three-phase cycle: Internal Prediction (P_int), Verification (V), Update/Cycle (D_cyc) | §3.1.1 |
| **Def 5** | Minimal Functional Requirements | Integrated capabilities stipulated for the full adaptive loop: $b_m$ (state representation/distinction), $b_p$ (predictive generation via $M_t$), and $b_v$ (verification plus update initiation). $D_{cyc}$ is a phase, not a fourth capability. | §3.1.2 |
| **Def 5a / Prop 5a / Cor 5a.1** | Protocol-Relative Functional Support | A registered role is response-active exactly when its admissible isolated typed-null replacement changes a finite protocol law; loss of qualification, baseline collapse, and cross-carrier transport require their separate stated hypotheses. | §3.1.2 |
| **Def 6** | Reflexive Interaction Dynamics (RID) | Outcome-dependent deterministic or probabilistic update formalism; updates may be noninvertible, outcomes are context-indexed, and predictive instability/regress requires the counter-predictive-response hypothesis of Lemma 2 | §3.2.1 |
| **Def 7** | Predictive Performance (PP) | $PP(t) = 1/(1+k_{PP}\cdot PE(t))$, mapping $PE \in [0,\infty)$ to $PP \in (0,1]$; higher PP = better prediction | §3.3.1 |
| **Def 8** | Space of Becoming | Task-, score-, window-, baseline-, and excitation-indexed open interval $(\alpha,\beta)$ with $0<\alpha<\beta<1$; no system-independent relation to an $\alpha_{SPAP}$ follows | §3.3.5 |
| **Def 9** | Self-Referential System | System whose state or dynamics depend upon a model representing aspects of itself | §4.1.1 |
| **Def 10** | Property R (Computational Richness) | Capacity to represent, simulate/reason, evaluate predicates, and compose retained finite programs with logical memory and Boolean post-processing | §4.1.2 |
| **Def 11** | Dynamic Self-Reference Operator (DSRO) | Computable function whose output depends on provability of statements about itself | §4.2.1 |
| **Def 12** | Logical Indeterminacy | Class-level failure of universal exact self-prediction on the stated SPAP branch and of total uniform $\mathsf{TERM}$ decision on the coded RID simulation branch; it does not imply eventwise stochasticity or general self-ignorance | §4.4 |
| **Def 13** | Operational Threshold (C_op) | Task-indexed infimum of $C_P$ over qualifying full-loop implementations; attainment is separate | §5.1 |
| **Def 14** | Baseline Logical Gate Error | Inherent error probability p_err,0 of uncorrected ND-RID implementation | App A.0.2 |
| **Def 15** | Principle of Compression Efficiency (PCE) | Declared objective on a specified comparison class, charging registered costs against predictive benefit; existence of a minimizer and convergence of physical dynamics require separate attainment and dynamical certificates | §6.1 |
| **Def 15a** | PCE-Attractor Branch | Branch on which $V$ attains its infimum, the global-minimizer quotient is a singleton, the selected orbit has the required flat U(1)-sector SLD-QFI spectrum, and the constrained rate-level minimum is cap-active; stability or convergence further requires the declared adaptation-law certificate | §6.1 |
| **Def 16** | Reliability Cost Contribution | V_rel(p_err) = λR(C_err(p_err)), cost of error correction overhead | App A.0.3 |
| **Def 17** | Effective Complexity | C_eff(p_err) = C_alloc · P_succ(T, p_err), complexity reduced by errors | App A.0.4 |
| **Def 18** | Minimum Complexity for Target PP | C_PPC(PP_target): minimum C_P to achieve PP_target | §6.3 |
| **Def 19** | Error Penalty Cost | V_err(p_err) = Γ₀ · T · C_alloc · [PP(C_alloc) − PP(C_eff)], performance loss from errors | App A.0.5 |
| **Def 19a** | Effective Complexity Scale $C_s$ | E-fold scale of the Law of Prediction: $C_s = \hat{C}_{target}/\kappa_{\mathrm{eff}}$ | §6.3 |

A compatible tuple has a common finite-cost PPI-physicalizable carrier when a common-realization-complete record over $q$ carries $\mathsf{base\mbox{-}exhausted}$ source status and supplies a nonempty essential preimage.

(\mathsf P,\mathcal R_{\mathfrak T},U_{\mathfrak T},V_{\mathfrak T}),

### RID Properties and Complexity Dynamics (Sections 3.2, 4.5)

**Summary of Lemma 2 (Properties of RID)** [§3.2.2]
The structure of Reflexive Interaction Dynamics (Definition 6) inherently implies:

| Property | Description | Implication |
|:---------|:------------|:------------|
| **(i) Potential Irrecoverability** | State transformation $T/T_{prob}$ may not be invertible | Pre-interaction state cannot always be recovered |
| **(ii) Information Context Shift** | Outcome $o$ depends only on $(x_n, y_n)$; resulting $x_{n+1}$ defines new context | Information is context-specific |
| **(iii) Predictive Instability/Regress** | Counter-predictive dynamics can defeat accurate self-prediction | Foundation for SPAP |

**Summary of Remark 1 (Distinct Roles of $\beta_0$, $\beta$, and $\alpha_{SPAP}$)** [§3.3.4]

| Quantity | Role | Source | Quantitative consequence |
|:---------|:-----|:-------|:-------------------------|
| $\alpha$ | Matched-baseline lower benchmark | Theorem 8 | Strict expected super-chance performance gives $PP>\alpha$ |
| $\beta_0$ | Pathwise excitation ceiling | Theorem 9 | $PP\le\beta_0$ on the excitation-certificate branch |
| $\beta$ | Registered strict upper endpoint and, on Theorem 19's exact branch, response-law asymptote | Definition 8; Theorem 19 | Finite complexity gives $PP<\beta$; logarithmic divergence requires the exact response law |
| $\alpha_{SPAP}$ | Task-specific quantitative boundary only after an additional registered performance theorem | Theorems 10–11; Theorem 14 | SPAP alone gives no system-independent scalar; Theorem 14 is certificate-gated |

On a joint Theorem-9/Theorem-19 branch, consistency requires $\alpha<\beta\le\beta_0$. No ordering between $\beta$ and a task-specific $\alpha_{SPAP}$ follows without a bridge identifying the same system, task, score, and window.

**Summary of Theorem 13 (Complexity Growth in Self-Modeling)** [§4.5.1]
Recursive self-modeling with minimum per-level overhead $k > 0$ yields:
$$
C(M_n) \ge c_0 + nk = \Omega(n)
$$
Complexity grows at least linearly with recursion depth.

**Summary of Theorem 14 (Predictive Complexity Divergence Near $\alpha_{SPAP}$)** [§4.5.2]
Fix a registered task, score, admissible class, and quantitative boundary $\alpha_{SPAP}$, and assume the Bernoulli reduction, independence, confidence, and operation-count certificate $\mathfrak C_{B.2}$. As $\delta_{SPAP}:=\alpha_{SPAP}-\alpha\downarrow0$,
$$
C_{\text{uni}}(\delta_{SPAP}) = \Omega\left(\frac{\log(1/\delta_{SPAP})}{\delta_{SPAP}^2}\right).
$$
The same asymptotic lower bound transfers to $C_{pred}(\alpha)$ only when a separate domination bridge proves $C_{pred}(\alpha)\ge C_{\text{uni}}(\delta_{SPAP})$ on the same task family.

The two-point testing certificate supplies the statistical resolution factor $\Omega(1/\delta^2)$ and the confidence requirement supplies the logarithmic factor. On that certified family the registered verification/update operation count has no finite uniform bound as $\delta_{SPAP}\downarrow0$. SPAP alone supplies neither a system-independent scalar boundary, a universal complexity divergence, nor a physical-energy divergence.

**Summary of Proposition 1 (Regulation Requirement under Uniform Uncontrolled Exit Risk)** [§3.3.7]
Let $\tau:=\inf\{n\ge0:PP_n\notin(\alpha,\beta)\}$. Assume that, without a regulatory or protective response, there exist $m\ge1$ and $p_{exit}\in(0,1]$ such that for every $k\ge0$,
$$
\mathbb E\!\left[
\mathbf1_{\{\tau>(k+1)m\}}
\mid\mathcal F_{km}
\right]
\le
(1-p_{exit})\mathbf1_{\{\tau>km\}}
$$
almost surely. Then $\Pr(\tau<\infty)=1$. Consequently, an implementation required by Axiom 3 to remain in $(\alpha,\beta)$ for all cycles almost surely must contain a regulatory or protective mechanism that invalidates this uniform exit-risk condition.

*Proof.* Let $A_k:=\{\tau>km\}$. Taking expectations in the assumed conditional inequality gives
$$
\Pr(A_{k+1})
=
\mathbb E\!\left[\mathbb E(\mathbf1_{A_{k+1}}\mid\mathcal F_{km})\right]
\le
(1-p_{exit})\Pr(A_k).
$$
Induction from $\Pr(A_0)\le1$ yields $\Pr(A_k)\le(1-p_{exit})^k$. Since $\{\tau=\infty\}\subseteq A_k$ for every $k$,
$$
0\le\Pr(\tau=\infty)\le(1-p_{exit})^k.
$$
Letting $k\to\infty$ gives $\Pr(\tau=\infty)=0$. ∎

## Part II: Philosophical Foundation

### Logical Sufficiency of the Cogito Starting Point (Section P.1.1)

The PU framework supplies a **formal candidate construction** for a consciousness-first interpretation of physics. It demonstrates mathematical articulability, while physical viability, unique realization, and empirical identification remain open:

| Achievement | How Demonstrated |
|:------------|:-----------------|
| **Mathematical formalization** | Axiomatization (POP, PCE), formal definitions (MPU, Perspectival States), rigorous derivations |
| **Conditional physics-like structures** | Quantum mechanics (§8), Lorentzian spacetime (§11), Einstein equations (§12), and Standard Model structures (App G, R) on their declared reconstruction and realization branches |
| **Quantitative rows** | $\alpha^{-1}$ as theorem-level Thomson output only with an accepted Thomson certificate and all-orders residual certificate; $\Lambda$, primordial product lock, baryogenesis, fermion masses, mixing angles, electroweak thresholds, and dark-sector quantities carried with their theorem, certificate, reference-convention, observational-inversion, validation, bridge-law, or model-layer status labels; theorem-level heat, zeta, eta, determinant, threshold, or spectral-action scalars must factor through the master zeta-index ledger with fixed finite-part and zero-mode prescriptions; registered numerical projections cannot be retuned after comparison |
| **Falsifiable protocols** | CC experimental tests (§13) with power analyses |
| **Hard-problem relocation** | A consciousness-first starting point removes the task of deriving awareness from a stipulated non-conscious primitive; phenomenal character, ontology, and physical realization remain separate open bridges |

**Paradigm Shift (Section P.1.1)**
The question shifts from "Can consciousness-based physics be rigorous?" to "Is this particular consciousness-first framework empirically correct?" This transition—from methodological exclusion to empirical evaluation—establishes the consciousness-first/idealist reading as a viable candidate interpretation. The Cogito remains the epistemic starting point of the minimal formal derivation; the consciousness-first or idealist ontology is developed in Appendix P as an interpretive branch.

### The Hard Problem and Idealism (Sections P.2.2–P.2.3)

**The Hard Problem of Consciousness** [Chalmers 1996]: Why do physical processes give rise to subjective experience? The explanatory gap between objective dynamics and phenomenal experience persists despite neuroscience progress.

**Idealism as Candidate Resolution (Section P.2.3)**
Traditional approaches face irreducible difficulties:

| Approach | Problem | Status |
|:---------|:--------|:-------|
| **Materialism** | Must explain how arrangements of matter produce qualia | Explanatory gap remains |
| **Dualism** | Must explain mind-matter interaction | Interaction problem unsolved |
| **Idealism** | Takes consciousness as primary | Relocates the emergence question; the phenomenal and physical-realization bridges remain open |

The Cogito gives idealism an epistemically economical starting point, and Occam's Razor is applied as the comparison of predictive yield per declared structural cost stated in Section 14.6.8. Identifying physical patterns with structures within consciousness remains an interpretive branch subject to PU's physical-realization and empirical gates.

**Dream Analogy as Illustration (Section P.2.3)**
Dreams demonstrate that consciousness can generate apparent realities with remarkable sophistication—entire experiential worlds arise as constructs of mind.

### The Cogito → Predictive Operation → SPAP Chain

**Summary of Foundational Certainty P.2.1 (Cogito)** [App P.2.1]

The Cogito is not an axiom or postulate. It is the self-verifying epistemic certainty that at least one locus of conscious awareness exists under methodological doubt. Its denial presupposes the awareness it denies, and it introduces no ontological commitment beyond the existence of conscious awareness.

**Summary of Definition P.3.1 (Predictive Operation)**
The next layer is not that the act of doubting is itself a prediction. Rather, the framework operationally characterizes the knowing/updating process relevant to finite-resource inquiry as a predictive map
$$
\pi : \mathcal{H}_t \to \mathcal{O}_{t+1},
$$
where accessible histories are mapped to anticipated outcome classes. The awareness certified by Foundational Certainty P.2.1 is predictive only insofar as it performs state updates $s_{t+1}=U(s_t,e_t)$ chosen to reduce predictive error under finite resource constraints. This structure is then formalized by the Fundamental Predictive Loop (Definition 4) and POP (Axiom 1).

**Verification in the Adaptive Predictive Class (Sections P.3.2–P.3.4; Definition 4)**
A Definition-4 adaptive loop carries a registered comparison between expected and realized responses and an outcome-conditioned update. A fixed forecast can instead be scored against an above-baseline rule without itself implementing an adaptive update loop. SPAP/RUD applies only after the relevant encoding, query, response, and closure hypotheses are supplied.

**Cogito-Relative Binary Epistemic Classification (Section P.2.4)**
The Cogito labeling is a two-class warrant convention: value $1$ denotes Cogito-grade certainty and value $0$ denotes propositions without that warrant. It is not by itself a physical bit, binary verification quotient, thermodynamic record, or ontological self/non-self substrate. Each such use requires a separately stated map and realization certificate.

**Summary of Proposition A.0.1 (Binary Necessity for Retained Verification Decisions)**
For a finite protocol with response set $R$ and declared retained acceptance set $A\subseteq R$, the update predicate is the characteristic function
$$
V_A(r)=\mathbf1_A(r)\in\{0,1\}.
$$
Scores, distances, likelihoods, confidence reports, and other protocol data may remain multi-valued.

**Summary of Proposition A.0.2 (Boolean Operations under Acceptance-Set Closure)**
If the retained protocol class is closed under complementary acceptance sets, finite intersections, and finite unions, then its characteristic predicates implement NOT, AND, and OR by
$$
V_{R\setminus A}=1-V_A,\qquad
V_{A_1\cap A_2}=\min(V_{A_1},V_{A_2}),\qquad
V_{A_1\cup A_2}=\max(V_{A_1},V_{A_2}).
$$

### Logical Infrastructure of Prediction (Appendix A.0.2)

The finite-time computation theorem requires three independent structural hypotheses; persistence or prediction alone does not supply them:

| Feature | Description | Role in Computation |
|:--------|:------------|:--------------------|
| **Composition Closure** | Outputs of one verification serve as inputs to subsequent ones | Enables circuit construction |
| **Logical Memory** | Predictions from cycle $t$ inform cycle $t+1$ | Provides state persistence |
| **Uniform Specification** | Models exhibit systematic, rule-based structure | Enables formal manipulation |

**Summary of Theorem A.0.1 (Functional Completeness and Universal Computation)**
The set $\{\mathrm{NOT},\mathrm{AND},\mathrm{OR}\}$ is functionally complete [Post 1921]. Given composition closure, logical memory, and uniform specification, every finite-time Turing-machine computation has an explicit Boolean-circuit simulation: encode the bounded configuration, build the Boolean next-configuration map from the transition table, and iterate it for the chosen time bound. The Church-Turing thesis is not used as a proof step; the closure is by direct finite circuit simulation.

**Summary of Corollary A.0.1 (Property R on the Uniform Predictive Class)**
Let a uniform predictive model class be closed under the registered Boolean constructions and finite composition, admit logical memory and arbitrarily large finite memory/composition depth, and carry a formal arithmetic coding finite strings, circuits, and bounded computations. Theorem A.0.1 then constructs a circuit simulation for every bounded computation in the class, and Corollary A.0.1 gives Property R relative to that arithmetic:
$$
\begin{aligned}
&\text{uniform scalable predictive class}+\text{registered coding/closure}
\to\text{Property R}
\to\text{SPAP diagonal limitation},\\
&(\mathrm{O1})\text{--}(\mathrm{O3})+(\mathrm{FC})
\to N_{\mathrm{vis}}^{\min}=8
\to K_0=3.
\end{aligned}
$$
A single finite predictive-cycle system, or the three logical operations without scalable memory, depth, uniformity, and arithmetic coding, does not discharge this theorem. The two displayed rows are independent. Transfer of the second row to MPU complexity additionally requires Definition 23, Hilbert distinguishability, and Corollary 3's complexity-capacity bridge.

**Logic from Retained Predictive Verification (Section P.3.4; Appendix A.0.2; Corollary P.16c.3)**
The Cogito supplies the foundational self-verifying predicate, and Proposition A.0.1 with Remark A.0.1.1 proves that a finite prediction protocol entering the update loop has a binary retained acceptance decision. Proposition A.0.2 derives the Boolean operations from the corresponding registered prediction constructions:

| Operation | Predictive implementation |
|:----------|:-----------------------------------|
| NOT | Complementary acceptance cut |
| AND | Joint verification predicate |
| OR | Alternative-acceptance predicate |

**Computational Simulation on the Uniform Predictive Class (Theorem A.0.1; Corollary A.0.1)**
Given composition closure, logical memory, uniform specification, arbitrarily large finite memory and depth, and a representing arithmetic, Theorem A.0.1 constructs Boolean circuits for every bounded Turing-machine computation and Corollary A.0.1 establishes Property R. This is a theorem-level derivation on the declared predictive class; it does not assert bivalence outside the retained predicate algebra or simulation without scalable finite resources.

### Predictionism (Appendix P.3.4)

Predictionism is an interpretive proposal that takes prediction as an ontological primitive. Its theorem-level content remains the finite operational results proved for declared predictive classes:

1. **Operational grounding:** retained physical claims require registered predictive protocols and responses.
2. **Self-reference scope:** SPAP supplies a class-relative diagonal limit under its closure hypotheses.
3. **Resource scope:** thermodynamic costs arise only for their registered physical implementations, including resets.
4. **Selection scope:** PCE compares a nonempty admissible class under a complete commensurate objective; attainment and strictness are additional gates.

Physical sectors are reconstructed only on their named carrier, realization, optimization, continuum, and calibration branches.

### From Cogito to Plurality (Appendix P.2.5)

**From Cogito to Plurality (Section P.2.5; Theorem P.1; Thesis P.2.5.2)**
The Cogito supplies one epistemic starting point but does not settle solipsism. Theorem P.1 proves only that one $\mathbb C^8$ MPU carrier cannot realize its declared SM-like anomaly-free chiral representation class; it does not prove that every single-MPU universe is inconsistent.

**Mathematical Challenge:** Embed the Standard Model chiral gauge structure into a single MPU Hilbert space $H_0 \cong \mathbb{C}^8$. For viability:
- **C1 (Predictive Anomaly Descent):** On the connected regular source branch with $Z_x[J]\ne0$, the 8-dimensional representation must have vanishing local/free gauge-anomaly class because gauge transformations are predictive-frame redundancies. Vanishing of the total class, including torsion or global anomalies, additionally requires the bordism-valued audit of Theorem X.8d.3 (Theorem X.8d; Theorem X.8d.3).
- **C2 (Chirality):** The representation must be complex to support chiral fermions; $G$ must contain non-Abelian factors $SU(2)$ and $SU(3)$
- **C3 (Simplicity):** $G$ is a compact Lie subgroup of $SU(8)$

**Result:** Under the representation and anomaly hypotheses of Theorem P.1, one carrier $\mathbb C^8$ does not realize the declared SM-like chiral sector. Realization of that sector therefore requires a larger carrier or multiple MPU carriers on the stated physical branch. Only after the separate postulate identifying an MPU with a locus of awareness is adopted does a multiple-MPU realization support a conditional plurality-of-awareness reading.

### The Distinction Framework (Section P.4)

**Protocol-Relative Distinguishability (Section P.4.1)**
For a declared protocol family, two candidates are operationally distinct when some registered response separates them. Failure of that protocol to distinguish candidates does not prove their ontic nonexistence. A physical bit additionally requires a carrier, encoding, retained record, and readout certificate.

**Consciousness and Registered Distinctions (Section P.4.2)**
The Cogito certifies the present self/non-self distinction. Treating consciousness as the physical producer of all operational distinctions is an interpretive thesis; each such distinction requires its own protocol, carrier, and sector certificate:

| Distinction Type | Description | PU Correspondence |
|:-----------------|:------------|:------------------|
| **Primary (Self/Non-Self)** | The Cogito establishes one certainty class relative to propositions not carrying that warrant | Cogito-relative epistemic classification (P.2.4), not a physical bit |
| **Operational** | Making a prediction and verifying it—a fundamental act of distinction | MPU as minimal distinguisher |
| **Informational** | Least visited-context log-capacity for the registered SPAP encoding under (O1)–(O3) and (FC) | Horizon Constant $K_0=3$ on that realization class; transfer to $C_P$ requires Corollary 3's bridge |

### Simulation Hypothesis Reframed (Section P.5.3–P.5.5)

**Simulation as Modeling Framework (Section P.5.3)**
The Simulation Hypothesis [Bostrom 2003] is reframed—not as a probability argument about computer simulations, but as a methodological tool. If consciousness is primary and reality is informational, then the universe can be modeled as a self-consistent information-processing system. A PCE optimization claim additionally requires a named nonempty response-equivalent comparison class, a complete commensurate objective, attainment, and the strictness needed for the claimed uniqueness.

**Constitutive-Simulation Thesis (Section P.2.3a)**
Section P.2.3a separately nominates an idealist ontological branch on which the simulators' jointly realized observation cycles constitute the simulated world and the MPU network is their proposed finite physical representation. Construction P.2.3a.2a and Theorem P.2.3a.2b prove a nonempty two-node formal branch with response-faithful carrier identity, finite Authentic boundaries, no-write observation, internal controls, unique world gluing, protocol contextuality on a distinct outcome layer, a discrete generator clock, counting trace, and one-use cut saturation; Corollary P.2.3a.2c proves the two-register minimum in its fixed-capacity exact-injection class. The bridge certificate $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{CS}}$ separately governs general physical realization, efficiency selection, unrestricted architecture comparison, observer trace and cosmology maps, physical CC attribution, and empirical identification. The Section P.5 simulation definitions remain the branch-independent architecture layer.

**Default Efficiency in Simulation Architecture (Section P.5.3a)**
Naturalistic simulation modeling assumes finite accessible resources over every finite operational domain. Therefore, among design-response equivalent architectures, uncompensated surplus is not the default. Extra cost may be retained when it buys robustness, observability, redundancy, security, experimentation, or novelty; response-null and value-null surplus is removed by the same no-surplus logic that governs PCE.

*Note:* Infinite simulated and nonsimulated classes cannot be compared by cardinality alone; a probability claim requires a declared measure and reference class. The PU simulation stance does not depend on that claim.

### Authentic vs. Synthetic Simulations (Section P.5.4)

**Summary of Definition P.5.1 (Synthetic Simulation)**
A modeling class with a registered external prediction map or intervention map for specified internal variables. Prediction and control are distinct; neither implies the other.

**Summary of Definition P.5.2 (Authentic Simulation)**
A modeling class with two independently certified boundaries:

1. **Epistemic boundary:** a class-relative diagonal nonprediction certificate satisfying SPAP's coding, query, response, and uniform-closure hypotheses.
2. **Control boundary:** a causal no-write certificate showing that the nominated external operations cannot alter the registered internal responses.

A read-only observation map is compatible with the control boundary. Structured novelty is a separate desideratum, not part of Definition P.5.2.

### The Observation Channel (Section P.5.5)

**Compatibility question:** If a simulation branch independently requires external readout while retaining a Control Boundary, can it export a registered record without changing any registered internal statistic? Purpose alone does not imply that readout objective.

**Summary of Definition P.5.3 (Observation Channel)**
On a branch with an independently declared external-readout objective, a design contract satisfying:

| Property | Requirement |
|:---------|:------------|
| **(i) External Accessibility** | External agents can extract information about internal states |
| **(ii) Internal Inaccessibility** | No internal system can extract predictive information: $\mathbb{E}[\Delta Q \mid E; M] = 0$ |
| **(iii) Non-Intervention** | Channel permits observation without modifying internal states |

**Summary of Theorem P.5.1 (Read-Only Channel and Reset-Ledger Separation)**
Under Definition P.5.3, external readout does not write to the internal dynamics. Any logically irreversible reset performed by the external observer obeys $Q_{\mathrm{reset}}\ge k_BT\,H(P\mid R)$ in the observer's thermodynamic ledger; no internal heat cost follows merely from the read-only map.

**Quantum Randomness and the Authenticity Model (Section P.5.5; Theorems P.5.1–P.5.2)**
On the declared instrument/kernel branch, intrinsic transition probabilities provide the stochasticity used by the authenticity model. This operational stochasticity is distinct from thermodynamic reset cost. A physical implementation obeys $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{phys}}\ge H_q(P\mid R)$ only when it performs a registered reset; an arbitrary interaction or observation channel has no automatic positive heat floor.

### Wigner's Puzzle Resolution (Appendix P.7.1)

**Problem:** Why is mathematics so effective in describing the physical world?

**Branch-indexed account:** Appendix P.7 proposes that mathematical and physical descriptions can use the same PCE variational grammar under different admissible contracts:

1. **Mathematics** supplies abstract structures and extremal problems within a declared mathematical class.
2. **Physics** supplies finite protocol-response realizations, resource ledgers, and empirical branch data.

A mathematical extremum becomes a physical candidate only after a finite PPI implementation exists and a nonempty physical comparison class with an attained PCE optimum is supplied. The shared grammar is therefore a conditional structural comparison, not a theorem that PPI/PCE determines every realized physical branch.

**Nonformal structural comparison at $M=24$.**
Several independently supplied mathematical branches use the integer $24$:

| Domain | Problem | Structure |
|:-------|:--------|:---------|
| Mathematics | Optimal sphere packing in 24D | Leech lattice $\Lambda_{24}$ |
| Physics model | Error correction on the registered length-24 branch | Golay code $[24,12,8]$ |
| Information comparison | Kissing number in 4D | $K(4)=24$ |

This table records a comparison among branch data; Proposition P.7.2 does not establish it or prove that the coincidence is necessary.

### Golden Ratio and Emergence Dynamics (Section P.7.3)

**Summary of Prediction Z.5 (Golden Ratio Crossover)**
The Hopkins–Stillinger–Torquato theorem (2010) proves the OSC-to-DLP implication for $1\le R\le\varphi$; it does not by itself require OSC/DLP decoupling or a dynamical transition above $\varphi$. If the MCC mode-channel relaxation branch is independently certified to follow this OSC$\to$DLP local-packing pathway during thermalization, then $\varphi$ is a candidate branch-local crossover marker.

| Constant | Traditional Role | Framework Role |
|:---------|:-----------------|:---------------|
| $c$ | Speed of light | Structure of causality |
| $\hbar$ | Quantum of action | Limit of determinism/action-entropy exchange |
| $G$ | Gravitational coupling | Spacetime curvature scale |
| $\varphi$ | Golden ratio | Conditional OSC$\to$DLP channel-crystallization marker |

**Interpretation:** The first three constants enter accepted physical branches as exchange or coupling scales. The golden ratio, if observed, marks the specified channel-relaxation branch rather than a primitive physical constant or universal lattice-proportion law.

**Golden Ratio and Biological Scaling Link (Section P.7.3)**
If $\varphi$ appears as a branch-local MCC crossover, then the shared content with biological packing examples is a branch-contract form of extremal packing under finite-resource constraints. This is not identity of biological and spacetime cost functions and does not make $\varphi$ a theorem-level spacetime constant.

*Status:* Branch-conditional falsifiable prediction; if falsified, main derivations ($M=24$, $D=4$, $\alpha_{\mathrm{em}}$) remain intact.

### Conditional Symmetry Selection under PCE (Appendix P.6.4)

PCE compares symmetry only after a group action, admissible comparison class, predictive utility, and resource functional are specified.

1. **Symmetric representative:** If group averaging remains admissible, preserves the registered predictive response, and weakly lowers the declared convex cost, PCE can select a symmetric representative.

2. **Symmetry breaking:** An asymmetric representative is selected only when its decrease in total PCE potential is established within the same comparison class. The electroweak value $\kappa_{EW}=38.5$ retains its Appendix-T branch and certificate status; it is not a universal cost of asymmetry.

3. **PCE-attractor branch:** Flat U(1) SLD-QFI is an explicit branch clause of Definition 15a. A theorem identifying it with maximal symmetry requires a declared symmetry action and a comparison or uniqueness proof.

### Spacetime as Finite-Response Geometry (Appendix P.8)

**Summary of Thesis P.8.1 (Conditional Spacetime Interpretation)**
Appendix P.8 interprets spacetime as the effective geometry of predictive coherence on branches carrying all required operational certificates. Spatial geometry requires causal-response, metric-reconstruction, regularity, and continuum data. Lorentzian signature requires the Appendix O principal-symbol and attained-cone package; dimensionality requires the Appendix Z mode-channel branch; temporal orientation requires the independent Appendix O path-measure certificate.

**Summary of Definition P.8.2 (Spacetime as Certified Predictive Geometry)**
On such a branch, spacetime denotes the effective geometric structure reconstructed from the network's retained finite responses. The Golay code can enter an error-correction branch, but code data alone do not determine a metric, causal cone, dimension, or arrow.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.1 (Separated Operational Inputs)**
The Fundamental Predictive Loop supplies an order of operations $P_{int}\to V\to D_{cyc}$ inside a declared protocol. Finite cycle duration, synchronization, causal horizons, spatial reconstruction, and continuum closure are independent network or bridge inputs and inherit their local hypotheses.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.2 (Conditional Arrow of Time)**
Operational prediction precedes verification and update by definition. A physical probability arrow follows only for histories carrying forward and reverse measures on a common event algebra and the positive pathwise entropy-production certificate of Appendix O.

2. **Thermodynamic Ratchet:** On the declared binary-ancilla architecture, Lemma J.1 proves noninjectivity when its reachable-domain and prescribed-ready-output hypotheses hold. If that architecture executes a registered isothermal reset, Theorem 31 gives $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{reset}}=H_q(P\mid R)+\varepsilon_{\mathrm{diss}}\ge H_q(P\mid R)$. A positive cycle floor requires $H_q(P\mid R)\ge h_{\min}>0$, and a forward/reverse path-probability bound additionally requires the common-event-algebra and pathwise-certificate hypotheses of Theorem O.3.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.5 (Lorentzian Signature on the Appendix O Hyperbolic-Principal-Symbol Branch)**
Assume the Appendix O branch carries a rank-four positive continuum tensor, a nonvanishing entropy-selected time covector whose kernel has rank three, a second-order continuum principal symbol, and a nondegenerate hyperbolic cone coinciding with the operational causal frontier. Combining those data with Theorem Z.11's four-dimensional Euclidean response carrier yields an emergent metric of signature $(-,+,+,+)$. Finite propagation speed, $D=4$, and a thermodynamic arrow do not establish this signature without the principal-symbol and cone-identification hypotheses.

**Conditional Information-to-Structure Reading (Section P.8.9)**
The $24$-mode, Golay, Leech, and $D=4$ entries are independently gated branches sharing the integer $24$. Predictive recovery fixes the rate, an accepted $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{dist}}$ selects the Golay equivalence class, the Leech branch uses explicit gluing or the Gaussian Niemeier selector, and the dimensional branch uses a faithful shell, $24\le K(D)$, the $K(3)=12$ obstruction, the regular $24$-cell, and least-feasible support cost. Physical QEC still requires its registered noise, syndrome, recovery, threshold, and gate-overhead certificate.

### What is Life (Section P.8.9a)

**Summary of Definition P.8.9a.10.1 (Life in the PU Framework)**
On the declared biological-model branch, a living classification requires five registered records: a complexity threshold, a degradation-and-recovery certificate, an adaptive-response score improvement with resource ledger, a reproduction-and-inheritance test, and a finite heritable update kernel. Generational PCE reweighting follows only on Proposition P.8.9a.6's separate fixed-weight, faithful-inheritance, no-flux branch.

**Summary of Thesis P.8.9a.1 (Operational Separation of Persistence Resources)**
Low thermodynamic entropy, mutual information, channel capacity, code redundancy, and repair work are distinct quantities. A concrete model may relate them only through a stated state space, channel, code, and work ledger.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.2 (Conditional Rate–Budget Identity)**
On the conjunction of the residual-budget same-family saturation branch, where $C_{\max}^*=\ln d_0-\varepsilon_0=2\ln2$ for $(d_0,\varepsilon_0)=(8,\ln2)$, and the predictive-recovery MacWilliams branch selecting the binary $[24,12,8]$ code with rate $R=1/2$,
$$
(1-R)\,C_{\max}^*=\ln2=\varepsilon_0.
$$
The equality is arithmetic on the two branch values; it does not identify twelve physical interface modes as parity modes, equate structural log-cardinality with reset heat, or show that every SPAP cycle implements a reset.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.3 (Structural Parameter Correspondence)**
The 144 = 12² structural count is retained only for the PU-internal finite-response structures:
- Golay parity matrix $P\in\mathbb F_2^{12\times12}$
- Active-inactive coupling count $bM=6\times24=144$
- Interface constraint count $12\times12=144$

The biological comparison is not part of the 144-count theorem. It enters separately as an error-tolerant persistence analogy: the genetic code maps 64 codons to 20 amino acids plus termination under a redundancy pattern shaped by translation robustness and evolutionary accessibility.

**Genetic Code as Error-Tolerant Organization (Sections P.8.9a.3–P.8.9a.4; Proposition P.8.9a.1)**
The genetic code's redundancy maps 64 codons to 20 amino acids plus a termination class. It functions as biological error-tolerant organization through wobble and chemical-neighborhood robustness, not as a formal Shannon-Hamming linear code with a guaranteed minimum distance.

**Summary of Proposition P.8.9a.6 (Conditional Generational-PCE Representation of Fixed-Weight Selection)**
On a registered biological PCE branch contract with a complete retained ledger and on the finite-type, time-independent, faithful-inheritance, no-flux branch with
$$
w_i=a\exp\!\left[-\lambda_{\mathrm{gen}}V_{\mathrm{gen}}(i)\right],
$$
normalized selection concentrates on the global minimizers of $V_{\mathrm{gen}}$ within the initial support. This is a conditional generational-PCE representation, not an unconditional identity between evolution and PU adaptation.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.6a (Exact Normalized-Selection Correspondence)**
For $Z(p,w)=\sum_jp_jw_j>0$,
$$
p_i^+=\frac{p_iw_i}{Z(p,w)}
$$
is simultaneously normalized selection, a Bayesian posterior for likelihoods proportional to $w_i$, and multiplicative weights with $w_i=e^{-\eta\ell_i}$. Mutation composes an additional stochastic kernel, general frequency dependence preserves only the one-step identity, and equation equality alone establishes neither PU functional-role identity nor SPAP self-reference.
The proposition is a conditional fixed-weight selection representation on a registered biological PCE branch; it does not identify reproductive fitness with predictive capacity without the stated constitutive map.

### Pareto Signature (Section P.8.9a.11)

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.11.2 (Single-System Pareto Disproportion)**
For any predictive system at PCE equilibrium with relative complexity $x^* > 0$, the fraction of equilibrium value realized at effort fraction $f \in (0,1)$ satisfies $\rho(f; x^*) > f$, with $\rho \to 1$ as $x^* \to \infty$.

**Summary of Remark P.8.9a.11.1 (80/20 Depth)**
The common 80/20 ratio corresponds to the definite dimensionless equilibrium depth $\kappa_{\mathrm{eff}} x^* \approx 8.0407523768$.

**Summary of Corollary P.8.9a.11.3 (Concentration Under PCE Optimality)**
Under the stated DSC and interior-optimum hypotheses, decreasing scarcity $\lambda$ increases the optimal complexity $C^*$ and therefore increases the concentration ratio $\rho(f;x^*)$ at fixed $f$.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.11.5 (Pareto Tail from Multiplicative PCE Adaptation)**
Under the Multiplicative PCE Noise Hypothesis, the stationary distribution of complexity excess across a population of PCE-adapted aggregates has upper-tail index $\zeta = 1 + 2k/\sigma^2 > 1$, where $k$ is the local mean-reversion rate and $\sigma$ is the multiplicative noise amplitude. The theorem also gives the lower-barrier normalization and truncated inverse-gamma moments.

**Summary of Corollary P.8.9a.11.5a (Conditional Taylor Law)**
For fixed-shape stationary inverse-gamma scale families with common scaled floor and $\zeta>2$, the variance-mean relation satisfies $\operatorname{Var}(\nu)\propto \mathbb E[\nu]^2$.

**Summary of Proposition P.8.9a.11.6 (Small-Noise Inequality Scale)**
In the local small-noise regime, the logarithmic fluctuation approximation gives $G = 2\Phi(\sigma/(2\sqrt{k})) - 1 + O(\sigma^3/k^{3/2})$.

**Summary of Corollary P.8.9a.11.7 (Unified PCE Signature Across Domains)**
Collects the within-system concentration, population Pareto tail, conditional Taylor scaling, and local small-noise inequality signatures under their stated hypotheses.

**Summary of Remark P.8.9a.11.2 (Universal-Pattern Boundary)**
Separates the conditional Pareto/Taylor consequences proved in Section P.8.9a.11 from additional domain hypotheses needed for Zipf, $1/f$, KWW/stretched-exponential, Benford, criticality, log-periodic, and Kleiber/fractal-biological signatures.

**Summary of Corollary P.8.9a.11.8 (Tier-4 Biological-Fractal Status Boundary)**
States that biological fractal and allometric signatures require an appended biological transport or branching package; PCE may optimize such a package but does not by itself fix the fractal dimension or Kleiber exponent.

### Conditional Universal Patterns (Section P.8.9a.12)

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.1 (Conditional Zipf-Mandelbrot Law from Logarithmic PCE Cost)**
Shows that Gibbs weights with logarithmic ranked PCE cost $E_r=E_0+\gamma\log(r+r_0)$ give $p_r\propto(r+r_0)^{-\beta\gamma}$, with infinite-rank normalizability exactly when $\beta\gamma>1$.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.2 (Rank-Pareto Zipf Bridge)**
Shows that a Pareto survival tail $\mathbb P(X>x)=(x_0/x)^\zeta$ gives the rank-size curve $x_r=x_0(N/r)^{1/\zeta}$; for Theorem P.8.9a.11.5 this exponent is $1/(1+2k/\sigma^2)$.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.3 (Conditional $1/f$ Spectrum from Log-Flat PCE Relaxation Modes)**
Shows that a log-flat relaxation-rate measure $A\,d\lambda/\lambda$ over $[\lambda_{\min},\lambda_{\max}]$ gives $S(\omega)\sim\pi A/\omega$ in the intermediate window.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.4 (Stretched-Exponential Relaxation from Stable-Subordinated PCE Modes)**
Shows that a stable-subordinated mixture of exponential relaxation modes has the KWW form $\Phi(t)=\exp[-(t/\tau)^\alpha]$ for $0<\alpha\le1$.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.5 (Benford Mantissas from Multiplicative PCE Phase Mixing)**
Shows that uniform logarithmic mantissas give Benford probabilities $\log_b(1+1/d)$ and that a non-lattice multiplicative phase process converges to that law.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.6 (Log-Periodic Corrections from Discrete Scale Invariance)**
Shows that discrete scale covariance $F(\lambda x)=\lambda^\alpha F(x)$ implies $F(x)=x^\alpha P(\log x/\log\lambda)$ with period-one $P$ and Fourier log-periodic corrections.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.7 (Conditional Kleiber Scaling on the $D_{\mathrm{space}}=3$ Biological Transport Branch)**
Shows that a terminal-invariant, space-filling, area-preserving biological transport network with mass proportional to network volume gives $B\asymp M^{D/(D+1)}$, hence $B\asymp M^{3/4}$ on $D_{\mathrm{space}}=3$. The transport, terminal-invariance, space-filling, and area-preservation hypotheses are branch data; POP/PCE may optimize over such branches but does not by itself determine the Kleiber exponent.

**Summary of Corollary P.8.9a.12.8 (Tier-4 Biological Fractal Scaling)**
Shows that an appended self-similar biological branching package with copy number $n$ and length ratio $r$ gives $D_f=\log n/\log(1/r)$ over its scale window.

**Summary of Theorem P.8.9a.12.9 (Standalone Taylor Law for Fixed-Shape PCE Scale Families)**
Shows that every fixed-shape scale family $X_a=aY$ with finite nonzero mean and variance satisfies $\operatorname{Var}(X_a)=(\operatorname{Var}(Y)/\mathbb E[Y]^2)\mathbb E[X_a]^2$.

### Authentic Simulation Framework (Appendix P.5)

**Authentic vs. Synthetic Simulations (Definitions P.5.1–P.5.2)**

- **Synthetic simulation:** carries a registered external prediction or intervention map for nominated internal variables; prediction and control remain distinct.
- **Authentic simulation:** carries both a class-relative SPAP nonprediction certificate and a causal no-write certificate for nominated external operations. Read-only observation is not excluded, and genuine novelty is not inferred from either boundary.

**Quantum Randomness as Authenticity Signature (Section P.5.5; Theorems P.5.1–P.5.2)**
Quantum randomness requires the instrument/kernel theorem; an interaction has no automatic $\ln2$ heat charge. Physical reset cost is governed separately by $H_q(P\mid R)$ on a registered reset branch.

### Entropy Unification (Thesis P.6.1, Section P.6.5)

**Summary of Thesis P.6.1 (Conditional Entropy-Ledger Comparison)**
Appendix P.6.5 compares SPAP, Shannon, thermodynamic, von Neumann, and Bekenstein-Hawking ledgers through a common counting interpretation only after the domain-specific states, protocols, unit maps, and bridge hypotheses are supplied. The structural SPAP reference is $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$; it is not an automatic physical heat or area quantum.

**Five Entropy Ledgers:**

| Entropy domain | Formula | Required scope |
|:---------------|:--------|:---------------|
| **Registered binary structural ledger** | $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$ | Log-cardinality of the declared binary quotient; no reset heat follows |
| **Gibbs--Shannon retained ledger** | $S_{\mathrm{ret}}(p)=-k_B\sum_i p_i\ln p_i$ | Declared finite protocol partition and retention map |
| **Thermodynamic reset ledger** | $\langle Q_{\mathrm{bath}}\rangle/(k_BT)\ge H_q(P\mid R)$ | Registered reset; equality requires Landauer saturation |
| **von Neumann ledger** | $S_{vN}(\rho)=-k_B\operatorname{tr}(\rho\ln\rho)$ | Declared quantum state and retained algebra |
| **Horizon ledger** | $S_{\mathrm{rel}}/k_B=c^3\mathcal A/(4G_{\mathrm{op}}\hbar)+o(\mathcal A)$ on E.6's saturation branch; $S_{BH}$ and measured $G$ require two further bridges | Operational theorem plus independently gated physical identification |

**Selected-Point Counting Identity**
For the specified active reference state $\rho_{\mathcal A}=I_2/2$,
$$
S_{vN}(\rho_{\mathcal A})
=-k_B\operatorname{tr}\!\left(\frac{I_2}{2}\ln\frac{I_2}{2}\right)
=k_B\ln2
=k_B\varepsilon_0.
$$
This is a numerical state-counting identity at the registered binary point. It is not an entropy-conversion theorem and does not equate structural log-cardinality with bath heat or horizon entropy.

**Exchange-rate ledger (nonformal comparison)**
The fundamental constants serve as inter-domain exchange rates:

| Conversion | Formula | Physical Meaning |
|:-----------|:--------|:-----------------|
| Logical ↔ Thermodynamic | S_thermo = k_B ε | Boltzmann's constant converts nats to J/K |
| Information ↔ Heat | $Q/(k_BT)\ge H_q(P\mid R)$ | Conditional Landauer reset; equality requires quasistatic saturation |
| Information ↔ Geometry | I · L_P² = I · Gℏ/c³ | Planck area converts information to geometric area |

**Summary of Theorem 7.5.2 (Conditional Entropy and Horizon-Calibration Chain)** [§7.5.3]
The conditional chain begins only after its registered reset-support, geometric-density, saturation, horizon-identification, and calibration premises are supplied; SPAP alone supplies none of them.

$$
\boxed{
\text{reset-support certificate}
\xrightarrow{\text{Prop E.2a}}
C(\mathcal E_N)\le\ln d_0-\ln2
\xrightarrow{\text{Thm E.3}}
N_{\mathrm{eff}}=\sigma_{\mathrm{eff\,link}}\mathcal A+o(\mathcal A)

S_{\mathrm{rel}}\le k_B\sigma_{\mathrm{eff\,link}}C(\mathcal E_N)\mathcal A+o(\mathcal A)
}
$$
Equality and the notation $S_{\mathrm{rel}}=k_B\mathcal A/(4L_{P,\mathrm{op}}^2)+o(\mathcal A)=k_Bc^3\mathcal A/(4G_{\mathrm{op}}\hbar)+o(\mathcal A)$ require the capacity-achieving, entropy-saturating, additive-ledger, and positive-normalization branch of Theorem E.6.

| 4 | Lemma E.1 | Nonzero refresh $\Rightarrow f_{RID}=1-p < 1$ | Contractivity from refresh component |

**The $\varepsilon$ Ledger (Section 7.5.5)**
The symbol $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$ is a structural value on the registered binary fixed-ready-state branch. It is not a universal entropy-production quantum.

| Ledger | Statement |
|:-------|:----------|
| **Structural support** | Definition J.1 registers the binary quotient and Theorem J.1 gives $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$; Lemma J.1 separately proves conditional noninjectivity. |
| **Physical reset** | Theorem 31 gives $\varepsilon_{\mathrm{reset}}\ge H_q(P\mid R)$ only for a registered reset; a positive uniform floor requires $H_q(P\mid R)\ge h_{\min}>0$, and equality with $\ln2$ requires the conditionally uniform, no-side-information, zero-overhead branch. |
| **Active carrier** | Theorem Z.1 gives $a=2$ only from the sharp two-record, entropy-capacity, and no-surplus hypotheses; $\varepsilon_0$ alone does not fix $a$. |

On the conjunction of the active-carrier and downstream interface/code branches,
$$
a=2\Rightarrow M=24,
$$
and the $[24,12,8]$ code requires its separate predictive-recovery MacWilliams gate. The structural, heat, carrier, interface, and code ledgers are not interchangeable.

**Conditional Error-Correction Interpretation (Stage 4a)**
The predictive-recovery rate gate fixes $k=12$; only an accepted $\mathfrak C_{\mathrm{dist}}$ then selects the classical $[24,12,8]$ Golay equivalence class. Its parity count is not an entropy identity and does not imply that physical modes implement a decoder. Physical protection requires an encoder, error channel, syndrome instrument, recovery map, and non-double-counted resource ledger.

The Golay code rate $R=k/n=12/24=1/2$ means that an encoder maps 12 binary information symbols into a 24-coordinate codeword. It does not identify twelve physical interface modes as payload and twelve as protection; that interpretation requires the separately registered encoder, channel, syndrome, recovery, and resource ledgers.

### Reflexive Undecidability (RUD)

**Summary of Theorem A.2.3 (Fixed-Property RUD)**
For the one property
$$
\mathsf{TERM}(S)
\Longleftrightarrow
\exists n<\infty\text{ such that }b_n=1,
$$
no total interactive algorithm correctly decides every coded RID system $S_{e,w}$ that simulates one step of $e(w)$ per registered clock interaction and raises $b$ exactly on halting. The property is fixed before the decider is quantified.

**Summary of Theorem A.2.4 (Bounded Probabilistic Extension)**
Assume every interaction supplies a complete computable list of its finite outcome support and rational probabilities, and assume a computable finite runtime bound. Then no rational probabilistic interactive algorithm decides the same $\mathsf{TERM}$ property on every point-mass embedding of $S_{e,w}$ with success at least $1/2+\epsilon$ for any fixed rational $0<\epsilon<1/2$. Exact enumeration of the resulting finite rational interaction tree would otherwise decide halting.

### The Fundamental Question (Section P.3.5)

**Gödel Clarification.** Moving to a stronger axiom system can decide particular formerly undecidable statements but does not yield one uniformly complete predictor on the declared diagonal-closed Property-R class. Containment alone does not establish a global obstruction. PU proves only the separate unfolded finite-acyclic self-publication obstruction and the uniform encoded-procedure obstruction stated below.

**The Question:** "Why is there something rather than nothing?"

**Summary of Theorem P.3.5.1 (Containment)**
Any system $S$ that formulates questions about the totality $\mathcal{U}$ is contained in $\mathcal{U}$.

**Theorems P.3.5.2–P.3.5.3 (System and Predictive-State Inclusion)**
Any complete specification of $\mathcal{U}$ by $S$ must include $S$ (Theorem P.3.5.2) and $S$'s predictive states (Theorem P.3.5.3).

**Summary of Theorem P.3.5.4 (Self-Inclusive Structure)**
Any complete specification $\Sigma_S(\mathcal{U})$ of the totality is self-inclusive: the specification must include a specification of itself.

**Two Independent Impossibility Proofs:**

| Route | Mechanism | Result |
|:------|:----------|:-------|
| **Unfolded (Structural)** | Well-foundedness forbids $r \prec r$ | Theorem P.3.5.5: No unfolded complete specification exists |
| **Encoded (Diagonal)** | SPAP diagonal construction | Theorems 10–11: No universal predictor succeeds on all retained constructible self-referential systems |

**Summary of Theorem P.3.5.5 (Unfolded Regress Obstruction)**
Under unfolded self-inclusion requirements, perfect operational specification at any time $t^*$ is impossible. The contradiction $d \prec d$ (description as proper part of itself) is the formal core of the infinite regress intuition.

**Summary of Theorem P.3.5.6 (Insufficiency of a Non-State-Determining Specification)**
Under the stipulated complete-state specification criterion, a record that does not determine the totality state cannot constitute a complete specification. This does not show that every satisfactory semantic or explanatory answer must determine that state, nor does it extend the two obstruction theorems beyond their representation classes.

**Regime-qualified third category (Section P.3.5; effective Theorem P.3.5.9)**
The Fundamental Question is treated as meaningful while subject to two internal specification limits: no correct self-inclusive unfolded specification exists in the well-founded regime, and no single encoded predictive-specification procedure is uniformly correct over the declared operationally closed Property-R constructible class. These results do not exclude one correct realized encoded record or every semantic formulation, so “provably closed” applies only to the named regimes and quantifiers.

### Conditional Real-Spectrum Observable Statement (Appendix P.6.3)

A measurement protocol may register its quantitative outcomes in a specified real alphabet. PPI/PCE does not uniquely select that alphabet from finite response alone.

**Map entry: Theorem K.6.3 (Hermiticity under the Normal-Observable Hypothesis)**
Assume an observable on the finite retained Hilbert space is represented by a normal operator and that its possible outcomes are its spectral values. If every spectral value is real, the finite-dimensional spectral theorem gives
$$
O=U\operatorname{diag}(\lambda_j)U^\dagger
=
O^\dagger.
$$
Normality is essential; a nonnormal operator can have entirely real spectrum without being Hermitian.

### Conditional Temporal Engineering (Appendix P.10–P.11)

**Summary of Definition P.10.1 (Conditional Temporal Engineering)**
Temporal engineering is a registered ability to modulate a specified local cycle-rate or phase observable through a realized control map. Aggregate complexity or a nonzero CC label is only eligibility data. Existence requires a nonzero response, carrier, source, resource, locality, conservation, and calibration package.

**Conditional interaction interpretation (Appendix P.10–P.11)**
An awareness-first vocabulary relocates the classical interaction question but does not solve it mathematically. A physical influence claim requires Theorem 34's reachable strict-improvement and realization branch, Hypothesis 3's normalized response-active map, a source-exhaustive energy ledger, and an artifact-controlled observable response.

**Non-implication ledger for observer labels**

| Label | Additional certificate |
|:------|:-----------------------|
| Level 0 | attained minimal full-loop implementation on Definition 23's nonempty comparison class |
| Level 1 | aggregation plus registered constituent instrument events; no EOPR or mass conclusion follows from the label |
| Level 2 | Effective Operational Property R and an identifiable self-model splitting |
| Level 3 | Theorem 34's full strict-improvement, representation, attainment, stability, and physical-realization package |

No level label proves passage to another. On an accepted Level-3 branch, CC denotes a certified bias capacity; it is not generated merely by $C_{agg}>C_{op}$. Definition 4 supplies operational order only. A temporal duration, clock, carrier response, or identification of awareness with physical time requires its own calibration and Appendix F/L/O/S certificates.

### SPAP-Associated Time, Entropy, and Perspective (Section P.12)

**Summary of Theorem P.12.1 (Structural SPAP Ordering and Conditional Reset Branch)**
SPAP supplies an internal operation order and distinguishes a predictor record from its target. An entropy bound follows only on a declared architecture with registered classical resets and retained side information; no unconditional $k_B\ln2$ cost per cycle follows from SPAP alone.

**Summary of Theorem P.12.3 (Conditional Coexistence, Not Mutual Reconstruction)**
A certified protocol may jointly carry operational order $\mathcal{T}_{\mathrm{op}}$, a registered-reset entropy ledger $\mathcal{E}_{\mathrm{reset}}$, and an independently specified predictor–target identification $\mathcal{P}_{\mathrm{id}}$. No one of these data sets determines either of the others in general. Under a registered sequential-reset implementation,
$$
\Delta S_{\mathrm{bath}}\ge k_B\sum_jH_{q_j}(P_j\mid R_j).
$$
Any stronger bridge requires an additional injective-reconstruction or monotonicity hypothesis.

**Theorems P.13.27 and P.13.30 (Conditional Moonshine-Branch Vacuum Symmetry)**
On the Leech branch, if the orbifold and operational exact-symmetry hypotheses of Theorem P.13.27 hold, the selected vacuum VOA is $V^\natural$. Theorem P.13.30 then gives
$$
\operatorname{Aut}(V^\natural)=\mathbb{M}.
$$
This conditional vacuum-symmetry chain is separate from the non-equivalent protocol ledgers of Theorem P.12.3.

**Summary of Theorem P.12.2 (Non-Equivalence of the Four Impossibility Claims)**
The SPAP diagonal construction proves the declared impossibility of a universal predictor for its self-referential class. It does not by itself prove impossibility of time reversal, impossibility of future-directed records, or impossibility of one system instantiating another system's state or perspective. Those claims require, respectively, additional thermodynamic and boundary-condition hypotheses, a specified record protocol and causal ordering, and a subsystem-identification or no-isomorphism theorem.

### Monster Group as Vacuum Symmetry (Sections P.13.1–P.13.20)

**Summary of Theorem P.13.27 (Moonshine-Module Identification on the Leech-Lattice Branch)**
On the Leech-lattice branch singled out by the conditional selection criteria of Section P.13.10, if the selected vacuum is realized as an orbifold of $V_{\Lambda_{24}}$ by an involutive standard lift realizing the canonical $\mathbb{Z}_2$, then the exclusion of surviving positive-dimensional weight-one symmetry on that branch forces the underlying lattice involution to be $v\mapsto -v$, and the resulting holomorphic $c = 24$ VOA is the Moonshine module $V^\natural$.

**Summary of Theorem P.13.30 (Vacuum Automorphism on that Branch)**
$$
\text{Aut}(V_{\text{PCE}}) = \mathbb{M}
$$
The Monster group $\mathbb{M}$ is the automorphism group of the branch identified with $V^\natural$.

**Derivation Chain P.13.31 (SPAP to Monster, conditional branch)**

| Step | Structure | Origin |
|:-----|:----------|:-------|
| 1 | $\varepsilon_0=\ln2$ | Registered binary-support quotient (Definition 28; Definition J.1; Theorem J.1) |
| 2 | $a=2$ | Theorem Z.1 sharp-record, entropy-capacity, and no-surplus branch |
| 3 | $M = 24$ | QFI interface modes (Thm Z.5) |
| 4 | $\Lambda_{24}$ | Unique minimizer of the registered Gaussian theta-cost on the certified finite Niemeier class |
| 5 | $V^\natural$ | Moonshine-module identification on the Leech branch (Def P.13.2, Thm P.13.19, Thm P.13.24, Thm P.13.27) |
| 6 | $\mathbb{M}$ | Vacuum automorphisms on that branch (Thm P.13.30) |
| 7 | Moonshine | $j(\tau) - 744$ coefficients |
| 8 | Monstrous Moonshine | Conway-Norton correspondence recovered on the stated Moonshine branch |

**Precision Cost Principle (Appendix P.13; Definition P.13.2; effective Theorem P.13.19)**
Let $K$ be a compact positive-dimensional control region. If every sufficiently fine resolution uses one of $N_K(\delta)$ equiprobable stored labels, resets that label once per cycle with no retained side information, and charges $c_0>0$ per nat, then $V_{eff}^{sym}(K)=+\infty$ as $\delta\downarrow0$. A finite group has finite cost under the analogous label/reset model. The theorem does not exclude positive-dimensional symmetries outside this registered-controller branch or select a sporadic vacuum group without the independent Moonshine-branch hypotheses.

**Monstrous Moonshine Branch Account (Appendix P.13; Theorems P.13.24–P.13.30)**
On the stated Moonshine branch, the Conway--Norton correspondence and the Moonshine-module construction mathematically organize Monster representations in the coefficients of $J(\tau)=j(\tau)-744$. Their common use of rank or central charge $24$ is a branch compatibility, not a derivation of the VOA or Monster group from the interface mode count or error-correction objective. A physical vacuum interpretation still requires the independent Leech, chiral-VOA, orbifold-lift, and realization certificates named above.
